Company Profile, Corporate Culture                                       集團簡介、企業文化

CORPORATE PROFILE                                                        集團簡介
Huabao International Holdings Limited and its subsidiaries (the          華 寶 國 際 控 股 有 限 公 司 及 附 屬 公 司（「本 集
“Group”), consisting of flavours and fragrances segment, aroma           團」）下設香精板塊、香原料板塊、煙用原料板
raw materials segment, tobacco raw materials segment, condiments         塊 及 調 味 品 板 塊，主 要 從 事 香 精、香 原 料、煙
segment, is mainly engaged in the R&D, production and sales of           用原料和調味品的研發、生產和銷售，並圍繞
flavours and fragrances, aroma raw materials, tobacco raw materials,     核心產業鏈進行多元化投資，是一家基於中國
as well as condiments. The Group also makes diversified investments      味覺系消費品市場的投資實控股集團。
around its core businesses. It is an industrial holding group based on
China’s taste-based consumer goods market.

Company Profile, Corporate Culture                                           集團簡介、企業文化
The Group is also one of the leading enterprises in China’s aroma raw        本集團還是中國香原料領域的領先企業之一，
materials field. The principal products include many natural aroma           主要產品包括多種天然香料和合成香料。其生
and synthetic aroma products. Its production capacity and sales              產能力和銷售額在行業內佔有重要市場份額，
revenue account for a significant market share in the industry, and its      生產工藝及產品質量均達到國內外領先水平。
production technology and product quality have reached the leading           於二零一五年，本集團成功收購香原料行業的
domestic and international level. In 2015, the Group successfully            龍頭企業鹽城春竹，於二零一七年，再次成功
acquired Yancheng Chunzhu, a leading enterprise in aroma raw                 收購江西香海，對本集團香原料板塊的建立和
materials industry. In 2017, the Group successfully acquired Jiangxi         布局起到了積極的作用。本集團將憑借自身在
Xianghai. The acquisitions played a positive role in the establishment       行業領先的地位優勢和強大的科研實力，打造
and layout of the Group’s aroma raw materials segment. The Group,            亞洲乃至全球領先的香原料生產基地。
by virtue of its own industry advantages of the leading position and
strong scientific research strength, plans to build a leading aroma raw
materials production base in Asia and even the world.

In 2018, the Group made strategic adjustments to its business.               二零一八年，本集團對業務進行戰略性調整，
Through the acquisition of Jiahao Foodstuff, the Group entered into          透過收購嘉豪食品，進入具備巨大增長空間的
the condiment market that is of great growth room, and also diversify        複 合 調 味 品 市 場，進 一 步 多 元 化 集 團 業 務 結
the Group’s business structure and sources of income. In addition, the       構，為 股 東 創 造 更 多 收 入 來 源。此 外，本 集 團
Group successfully disposed of the loss-making e-cigarette business          成功出售掉虧損的電子煙業務，優化集團財務
and optimised the Group’s financial resources and efficiency.                資源及使用效率。

At present, the Group has successfully established an industry chain         目 前，本 集 團 成 功 完 成 了「原 料、加 工、消 費」
with full coverage from raw materials, processing, to consumption,           一體化的全產業鏈覆蓋，在味覺系消費品市場
which brings the Group’s competitive strength in taste-based fast-           的 競 爭 實 力 進 一 步 增 強。本 集 團 將 繼 續 通 過
moving products industry to a new level. The Group will also combine         多資本平台，多合作渠道，及多種投資合作模
its industry expertise and integration capability with the social capital,   式將我們的產業知識、整合能力與社會資本結
through the multi-capital platforms, multi-collaboration channels, as        合，為股東創造更大價值。
well as multi-investment collaboration models, to create more value
for the shareholders.

                         STRATEGY
                         戰略
                         •      Adhere to the “concentric diversification strategy” and develop into the world’s leading taste-
                                based consumer product industrial holding group.

                         •      Consolidate the existing market of flavours and fragrances and explore new markets with
                                product and technology innovation

                         •      Seize the opportunity of integration in aroma raw materials industry and strives to achieve the
                                goal of “Being the world’s leader in the aroma raw materials industry”

                         •      Closely monitoring opportunities in emerging markets and expand the markets of tobacco raw
                                materials

                         •      Consolidate the Group’s leading role in the niche segment in the condiment industry, leverage
                                innovative sales platform and incentive measures to build condiment business as the Group’s
                                growth line

                         •      堅持「同心多元化戰略」，發展成為全球領先的味覺系消費品實控集團
                         •      穩固香精業務的存量市場，透過產品及技術創新，開拓增量市場
                         •      抓住香原料行業整合的機會，向「成為全球香料行業的龍頭」這一目標奮進
                         •      密切關注新興市場業務機會，拓展煙用原料市場範圍
                         •      鞏固在調味品市場細分領域的領先地位，利用創新型銷售平台及激勵機制，推進調
                                味品業務成為集團業績新的增長線
                                                      Huabao International Holdings Limited 華寶國際控股有限公司 2018 Annual Report 年報   9


Chairlady Statement                                                      主席報告書




                                   CHU Lam Yiu
                                  朱林瑤
                                  Chairlady & CEO
                                  主席兼首席執行官


Dear shareholders,                                                       各位股東，

In 2018, the global economy and financial markets were turbulent         二零一八年，受貿易保護主義抬頭、英國脫歐
due to the rise of trade protectionism, Brexit and risks in emerging     以及新興經濟體風險影響，全球經濟及金融市
economies. In 2018, China’s economic growth rate reached 6.6%,           場動盪不安。二零一八年，中國經濟增長率達
the lowest growth rate in 28 years. Against the backdrop of              到6.6%，創下二十八年來最低增速。在宏觀經
slowdown in the macroeconomy, the Group adhered to the strategy          濟放緩的大環境下，本集團堅持以味覺系為中
of concentric diversification focusing on the taste-based industry       心的同心多元化戰略，卓有成效地推進了業務
and successfully completed the strategic adjustment of its business      板塊的戰略調整。回顧期內，香精業務成功於

     of 9.5% year-on-year. In particular, the turnover of fast food and         營 業 額 較 快 增 長，快 餐 服 務 企 業 營 業 額 比 上
     specialty catering enterprises increased rapidly. The turnover of fast     年增長10.4%，餐飲配送及外賣送餐服務增長

Corporate governance refers to the rules and incentives by which the       企業管治乃引導及監控公司管理層之規則及動
management of a company is directed and controlled to maximize             力，以為股東取得已兼顧其他合法持份者之權
the profitability and long-term value of the firm for shareholders while   益的最大企業盈利及長期價值（ 英國Cadbury
taking into account the interest of other legitimate stakeholders (UK      報告1992）。董事會重視良好企業管治常規之
Cadbury Report 1992). The Board recognizes the importance and              重要性及益處，並已採納若干企業管治及披露
benefits of good corporate governance practices and has adopted            常規，力求提高透明度及問責水平。本公司將
certain corporate governance and disclosure practices aiming at a high     不斷致力於提升企業管治作為本身企業文化
level of transparency and accountability. The Company is committed         之一部份。
to continuously improving its corporate governance practices as part
of its own corporate culture.

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                      風險管理及內部監控
The Board has full responsibility for maintaining an integrated and       董事會全權負責維持健全和有效的集團風險
effective risk management and internal control systems of the             管理及內部監控系統，包括制定管理層架構及
Group, which includes the establishment of a defined management           其相關之權限。內部監控系統旨在協助達成本
structure with specified limits of authority. The internal control        集團業務目標、保護資產以防未經授權之使用
system is designed to assist in the achievement of business objectives    或處理、確保會計紀錄得以妥善保存，以便提
of the Group, safeguarding assets against unauthorized use or             供可靠的財務資料供內部使用或對外發放，並
disposition, ensuring the proper maintenance of accounting records        確保遵守相關法例與規則，為無重大失實陳述
for the provision of reliable financial information for internal use or   或損失提供合理的、非絕對的保證。內部監控
for publication and ensuring compliance with relevant legislation         系統已嵌入本公司營運和成為其文化之一，包
and regulation. The internal control system is designed to provide        括：
reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against material misstatement
or loss. The internal control system (embedded in the operations of
the Company and forms part of the Company’s culture) includes:

meeting
Pursuant to bye-law 58 of the bye-laws of the Company, shareholders         按本公司之公司細則第58條，於遞呈要求之日
holding at the date of deposit of the requisition not less than one-        期持有不少於賦有本公司股東大會表決權之本

in such requisition.

days before the proposed date of the SGM.                                   公司於香港的主要營業地點。

ACQUISITIONS AND DISPOSAL                                                   收購及出售

     The risk of down-stream industry control and policies on                   下遊行業管控機制及政策對煙草行業影響的
     tobacco industry                                                           風險
     Following the promulgation and implementation of the new                   繼 二 零 一 五 年 中 國 新《廣 告 法》的 頒 佈 和 實
     Advertising Law of China in 2015, the restrictions on cigarette            施，對煙草行銷的限制更加嚴厲，煙草市場行
     marketing have in turn become more rigid, and consequently the             銷的空間正變得越來越窄。另一方面，提稅順
     room for marketing the tobacco market becomes narrower. On                 價政策提高了捲煙的消費稅和批發價，均在一
     the other hand, the tax-induced price hike policy has raised the           定程度上抑制了捲煙消費需求的增長。隨著國
     consumption tax and wholesale price, and subsequently escalated            際衛生組織和各地區政府部門加強控煙政策，
     the consumption cost of cigarettes, which to a certain extent              以及民眾對健康控煙意識的不斷增強，不排除
     have suppressed the demand for cigarette consumption. With the             未來捲煙的產銷量進一步下滑，從而導致本公
     tightening of tobacco control policies by the international health         司煙草相關產品銷售收入及營業利潤下降。本
     organizations and local governments, and the increased awareness           公司會持續關注行業及政策變化對業務的影
     of the public on health and tobacco control, it is possible that the       響，適時調整業務及發展策略。
     production and sales volumes of cigarette will further decline in the
     future, which in turn will cause the reduction of sales income and
     operating profit of the tobacco-related products of the Company. In
     this regard, the Company will continue to pay attention to the impacts
     of industry and policy changes on the business, and will adjust our
     business and development strategies in a timely manner.

Dividend Policy                                                         股息政策
The Group is committed to sharing its development and achievements      本集團致力於透過積極穩健及可持續的派息
with shareholders through proactive, stable and sustainable dividend    政策，努力在符合股東期望與審慎管理資本之
policy. The Group would strive to strike a balance between meeting      間達致平衡，與股東共同分享公司的發展和成
shareholder’s expectations and managing funds prudently. When           就。在考慮派息政策時，本集團將綜合觀察宏
considering the dividend policy, the Group will comprehensively         觀經濟運行情況、行業競爭格局以及自身發展
observe the macro-economic operation, the competition pattern of        戰略，在確保公司具備充足營運資金以落實發
the industry and the Group’s own development strategy. Under the        展戰略的前提下，將盈餘的資金派發給股東，
premise of ensuring that the Group has sufficient working capital to    回報股東的支持。公司如派息，派息比率將維
implement the development strategy, the Group will distribute surplus   持在50%或以上。
funds to shareholders and reward their support for the Group. If the
Company pays dividends, the dividend payout ratio will remain at
50% or above.

DIRECTORS’ PERMITTED INDEMNITIES                                         董事允許的彌償保證
Pursuant to the Bye-laws of the Company and subject to the relevant      根據本公司的公司細則並受當中相關條文所規
provisions therein, every Director shall be indemnified and secured      限，每名董事就執行其作為本公司董事之職務
harmless out of the assets and profits of the Company from and           或與之相關的其他事宜而承擔或蒙受的所有

     The directors of the Company are responsible for the other                貴公司董事須對其他信息負責。其他信息包括
     information. The other information comprises all of the information       年報內的所有信息，但不包括綜合財務報表及
     included in the annual report other than the consolidated financial       我們的核數師報告。
     statements and our auditor’s report thereon.

     Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements does not cover       我們對綜合財務報表的意見並不涵蓋其他信
     the other information and we do not express any form of assurance         息，我們亦不對該等其他信息發表任何形式的
     conclusion thereon.                                                       鑒證結論。

     In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial statements,    結合我們對綜合財務報表的審計，我們的責任
     our responsibility is to read the other information and, in doing so,     是閱讀其他信息，在此過程中，考慮其他信息
     consider whether the other information is materially inconsistent with    是否與綜合財務報表或我們在審計過程中所
     the consolidated financial statements or our knowledge obtained in        了解的情況存在重大抵觸或者似乎存在重大
     the audit or otherwise appears to be materially misstated.                錯誤陳述的情況。

     If, based on the work we have performed, we conclude that there is        基於我們已執行的工作，如果我們認為其他信
     a material misstatement of this other information, we are required to     息存在重大錯誤陳述，我們需要報告該事實。
     report that fact. We have nothing to report in this regard.               在這方面，我們沒有任何報告。

                  losses are also eliminated unless the transaction                              損也予以對銷，除非交易提供證據

                  transferred. Accounting policies of the joint ventures                         企營的會計政策已按需要作出改

                  years.                                                                        銷。


            (e)   Patents                                                                 (e)   專利
                  Patents include purchased technology and skills                               專利乃購自第三方的技術並具有
                  acquired from third parties. They have a finite                               限定可使用期，按成本減累計攤銷
                  useful life and carried at cost less accumulated                              及及累計減值虧損列賬。攤銷以直
                  amortisation and accumulated impairment losses.                               線法將專利及技術的成本按其估
                  Amortisation is calculated using the straight-line                            計可使用年期3至10年計算。
                  method to allocate the cost of the technology and
                  skills over their estimated useful life of 3 to 10 years.

      2.9   Impairment of non-financial assets                                      2.9   非金融資產的減值
            Assets that have an indefinite useful life, for example                       不確定使用年期之資產（ 如商譽或尚未
            goodwill or intangible assets not ready to use, are not                       可供使用的無形資產）無需攤銷，但最少
            subject to amortisation and are tested annually for                           每 年 就 減 值 進 行 測 試。各 項 資 產，當 有
            impairment. Assets that are subject to amortisation are                       事件出現或情況改變顯示賬面值可能無
            reviewed for impairment whenever events or changes in                         法收回時就減值進行檢討。減值虧損按
            circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may not                       資產之賬面值超出其可收回金額之差額
            be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised for the                      確認。可收回金額以資產之公允價值扣
            amount by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds                           除銷售成本或使用價值兩者之較高者為
            its recoverable amount. The recoverable amount is the                         準。於 評 估 減 值 時，資 產 按 可 分 開 識 辨
            higher of an asset’s fair value less costs to sell and value                  現金流量（ 現金產出單位）的最低層次組
            in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment, assets                      合。除 商 譽 外，已 蒙 受 減 值 的 非 金 融 資
            are grouped at the lowest levels for which there are                          產在每個報告日期均就減值是否可以撥
            separately identifiable cash flows (cash-generating units).                   回進行檢討。
            Non-financial assets other than goodwill that suffered
            an impairment are reviewed for possible reversal of the
            impairment at each reporting date.

      2.12 Offsetting financial instruments                                        2.12 抵銷金融工具
            Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net                      當有法定可執行權利可抵銷已確認金
            amount reported in the statement of financial position                       額，並 有 意 按 淨 額 基 準 結 算 或 同 時 變
            when there is a legally enforceable right to offset the                      現資產和結算負債時，金融資產與負債
            recognised amounts and there is an intention to settle                       可互相抵銷，並在財務狀況表報告其淨
            on a net basis or realise the asset and settle the liability                 額。法定可執行權利不得依賴未來事件
            simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not                       而定，而在一般業務過程中以及倘公司
            be contingent on future events and must be enforceable                       或對手方一旦出現違約、無償債能力或
            in the normal course of business and in the event of                         破產時亦必須可強制執行。
            default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the Company or the
            counterparty.

            Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the                   的估計售價，減去適用的變動銷售開支
            ordinary course of business, less applicable variable selling                計算。
            expenses.

                  Ordinary shares are classified as equity. Incremental costs              普通股列為權益。直接歸屬於發行新股
                  directly attributable to the issue of new shares or options              或購股權的新增成本在權益中列為所得
                  are shown in equity as a deduction, net of tax, from the                 款的減少（ 扣除稅項）。
                  proceeds.

                  Where any group company purchases the Company’s                          如任何集團公司購入本公司的權益股本
                  equity share capital (treasury shares), the consideration                （ 庫存股份），所支付的對價，包括任何
                  paid, including any directly attributable incremental costs              直 接 所 佔 的 新 增 成 本（ 扣 除 所 得 稅），
                  (net of income taxes) is deducted from equity attributable               自歸屬於本公司權益持有人的權益中扣
                  to owners of the Company until the shares are cancelled                  除，直 至 股 份 被 註 銷 或 重 新 發 行 為 止。
                  or reissued. Where such ordinary shares are subsequently                 如股份其後被重新發行，任何已收取的
                  reissued, any consideration received, net of any directly                對價（ 扣除任何直接所佔的新增交易費
                  attributable incremental transaction costs and the related               用及相關受影響的所得稅）包括在歸屬

                  owners of the Company.

      2.17 Borrowings                                                              2.17 貸款
            Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of                    貸款按公允價值並扣除產生的交易費
            transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently                      用為初始確認。貸款其後按攤銷成本列
            carried at amortised cost; any difference between the                        賬；所得款（ 扣除交易成本）與贖回價值
            proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption                       的任何差額利用實際利率法於貸款期間
            value is recognised in the income statement over the                         內在收益表確認。
            period of the borrowings using the effective interest
            method.

            Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are                        設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部份或全
            recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the extent                    部融資將會很可能提取，該費用確認為
            that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be                 貸款的交易費用。在此情況下，費用遞延
            drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the                      至貸款提取為止。如沒有證據證明部份
            draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that                    或全部融資將會很可能被提取，則該費
            it is probable that some or all of the facility will be drawn                用資本化作為流動資金服務的預付款，
            down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity                   並按有關的融資期間攤銷。
            services and amortised over the period of the facility to
            which it relates.

            Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the                  除非本集團可無條件將負債的結算遞延
            Group has an unconditional right to defer settlement of                      至結算日後最少12個月，否則貸款分類
            the liability for at least 12 months after the end of the                    為流動負債。
            reporting period.

      2.18 Borrowing costs                                                         2.18 貸款成本
            General and specific borrowing costs attributable to                         直接歸屬於收購、興建或生產合資格資
            the acquisition, construction or production of qualifying                    產（ 指必須經一段長時間籌備以作其預
            assets, which are assets that necessarily take a substantial                 定用途或銷售的資產）的一般及特定貸
            period of time to get ready for their intended use or sale,                  款 成 本，加 入 該 等 資 產 的 成 本 內，直 至
            are added to the cost of those assets, until such time as                    資產大致上備妥供其預定用途或銷售為
            the assets are substantially ready for their intended use or                 止。
            sale.

            Investment income earned on the temporary investment                         就特定貸款，因有待合資格資產的支出
            of specific borrowings pending their expenditure on                          而臨時投資賺取的投資收入，應自合資
            qualifying assets is deducted from the borrowing costs                       格資本化的貸款成本中扣除。
            eligible for capitalisation.

                  the Group’s activities. Revenue is shown, net of                             後以及與集團公司取消銷售後的
                  discounts and after eliminating sales with the Group                         收 入。當 收 入 金 額 能 夠 可 靠 計 量
                  companies. The Group recognises revenue when the                             時；當未來的經濟利益很可能流入

                  it is probable that future economic benefits will flow                       特定標準時本集團會確認收入，詳
                  to the entity; and when specific criteria have been                          情如下所述。
                  met for each of the Group’s activities, as described
                  below.

                  Revenues are recognised when or as the control of                            收入在資產控制權轉移給客戶時
                  the asset is transferred to the customer. Depending                          確認。根據合同條款和適用於合同
                  on the terms of the contract and the laws that apply                         的法律，資產的控制可以隨時間或
                  to the contract, control of the asset may transfer                           在某個時間點進行轉移。如果集團
                  over time or at a point in time. Control of the asset                        的業績如下，資產的控制權將隨時
                  is transferred over time if the Group’s performance:                         間轉移：


                  •     provides all of the benefits received and                              •     提供客戶同時收到和消費的
                        consumed simultaneously by the customer; or                                  所有好處；或

      2.24 Research and development costs                                           2.24 研發成本
            Research expenditure is recognised as an expense as                           研究費用於產生時確認為支出。倘開發
            incurred. Costs incurred on development projects (relating                    中項目（ 有關設計及測試全新或經改良

            criteria are fulfilled:

            (c)   there is an ability to use or sell the intangible asset;                (c)   有能力使用或出售該無形資產；

                  generate probable future economic benefits;                                   生經濟效益；

                  to complete the development and to use or sell the                            成開發及使用或出售該無形資產；
                  intangible asset are available; and                                           及


            (f)   the expenditure attributable to the intangible asset                    (f)   於開發該無形資產時的成本能可
                  during its development can be reliably measured.                              靠計量。

            not exceeding five years.                                                     年。

3.    CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                          3.       會計政策變動
      The Group adopted HKFRS 9 and HKFRS 15 from 1 April 2018,                     本集團自二零一八年四月一日採納了香港財
      which result in changes in accounting policies (new policies are              務報告準則第9號和第15號，導致了會計政策
      described in Note 2.11 and Note 2.23) and adjustments to the                 （ 新 的 會 計 政 策 已 在 附 註2.11及 附 註2.23列
      amounts recognised in the consolidated financial statements.                  示）的改變和綜合財務報表確認金額的調整。


      In accordance with the transitional provisions in HKFRS 9,                    依據香港財務報告準則第9號的過渡性條款，
      comparative figures have not been restated. As a consequence,                 本集團未重述比較期間數據。因此，任何對金
      any adjustments to carrying amounts of financial assets or                    融資產或負債賬面價值的差額均在本年調整
      liabilities are recognised at the beginning of the current                    年初留存收益餘額。相應的，將賬面價值調整
      reporting period, with the difference recognised in opening                   的差異確認至期初保留盈餘，減值準備在比較
      retained earnings. Provisions for impairment have not been                    期間未被重述。
      restated in the comparative period, as well.

      The Group adopted HKFRS 15 using the modified retrospective                   本集團選擇使用修正過的追溯方法向香港財
      approach which means that the cumulative impact of the                        務報告準則第15號過渡，即根據首次執行該收
      adoption (if any) will be recognised in retained earnings as of 1             入準則時的累計影響數，在本年調整年初留存
      April 2018 and that comparatives will not be restated.                        收益餘額。本集團選擇了適用於已完成合同的
                                                                                    簡化處理辦法，沒有對在二零一八年四月一日
                                                                                    前已完成的合同進行重述。因此，集團未重述
                                                                                    比較期間數據。

4.    FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                             4.     財務風險管理
      4.1   Financial risk factors                                                 4.1   財務風險因素
            The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial                   本集團的活動面對多項財務風險：市場
            risks: market risk (including foreign exchange risk, cash                    風 險（ 包 括 外 匯 風 險、現 金 流 量 之 利 率
            flow interest rate risk, and fair value interest rate risk),                 風 險 及 公 允 值 之 利 率 風 險）、信 貸 風 險
            credit risk and liquidity risk. The Group does not have any                  及流動性風險。本集團無重大的價格風
            significant price risk. The Group’s overall risk management                  險。本集團的整體風險管理計劃專注於
            programme focuses on the unpredictability of financial                       財務市場的難預測性，並尋求儘量減低
            markets and seeks to minimise potential adverse effects                      對本集團財務表現的潛在不利影響。
            on the Group’s financial performance.

            Risk management is carried out under the policies                            風險管理按照董事會批准的政策執行、
            approved by the Board of Directors. The Group identifies                     確定和評估財務風險。集團經營單位緊
            and evaluates financial risks in close co-operations with its                密合作以確定和評估財務風險。董事會
            operating units. The Board provides principles for overall                   為整體風險管理訂定指引，亦為若干特
            risk management, as well as policies covering specific                       定 範 疇 提 供 政 策，例 如 外 匯 風 險、利 率
            areas, such as foreign exchange risk, interest rate risk,                    風 險、信 貸 風 險，以 及 投 資 剩 餘 的 流 動
            credit risk and investing of excess liquidity.                               資金。

                        has been a significant increase in credit risk on an                      存續的報告期間是否會有信貸風
                        ongoing basis throughout each reporting period.                           險的顯著增加。在評估信貸風險是
                        To assess whether there is a significant increase                         否顯著增加時本集團將報告日時
                        in credit risk the Group compares the risk of a                           點資產發生壞賬的可能性與初始
                        default occurring on the asset as at the reporting                        確認時點發生壞賬的可能性進行
                        date with the risk of default as at the date of                           比較，同時也考慮公開且合理的前
                        initial recognition. It considers available reasonable                    瞻資訊。以下指標需要重點考慮：
                        and supportive forwarding-looking information.

                              in business, financial or economic conditions                            狀況、財務狀況和經濟環境

            4.2   Capital risk management                                                   4.2   資本風險管理
                  The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                             本集團的資本管理政策目的是保障其能
                  safeguard its ability to continue as a going concern in                         繼續營運，以為股東提供回報和為其他
                  order to provide returns for shareholders and benefits                          權益持有人提供利益，同時維持最佳的
                  for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital                       資本結構以減低資金成本。
                  structure to reduce the cost of capital.

                  In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the                       為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能
                  Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to                                會調整支付予股東的股息數額、向股東
                  shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue new                         分派的資本返還、發行新股或出售資產
                  shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                           以減低債務。


                  The operating cash inflows of the Group are sufficient for                      本集團經營活動產生的現金流可足夠用
                  investing activities including acquisition of new businesses.                   於投資活動包括收購新業務。


                  The Group monitors its capital risk based on the debt-                          本集團以資本負債比率監察其資本風
                  to-equity ratio. This ratio is calculated by dividing the net                   險。此比率乃按照債務淨額除以總資本
                  debt by the total owners’ equity. The Group’s net debt                          計 算。本 集 團 的 債 務 淨 額 為 總 貸 款（ 包
                  is calculated as total borrowings (including current and                        括流動及非流動貸款）減去現金及現金
                  non-current borrowings) less cash and cash equivalents.                         等價物。由於本集團的現金及現金等價
                  Since the Group’s cash and bank balance was larger than                         物 金 額 大 於 貸 款 金 額，本 集 團 於 二 零
                  borrowing amount, the Group’s debt-to-equity ratio is                           一八年十二月三十一日及二零一八年三
                  zero as at 31 December 2018 and 31 March 2018.                                  月三十一日的資本負債比率為零。

                  The following categorises financial instruments carried                         以下對以公允價值計量的金融工具進行

                  techniques within a fair value hierarchy. The different                         值技巧的級別釐定。不同層級的定義如
                  levels have been defined as follows:                                            下：

                  –     Inputs other than quoted prices included within                           －    除了第1層所包括的報價外，該資

                        either directly (that is, as prices) or indirectly (that is,                   直 接（ 即 例 如 價 格）或 間 接（ 即 源
